Browse · Terpenoids · β-carotene

β-carotene

CHEBI:17579 EXACT SEEDED antiviralenzyme inhibitor

What these status tags mean

This record is SEEDED: “Written from the committed inventories; no curator sign-off yet.” (the schema's definition).

Curation status describes a workflow state. SEEDED means the record entered through the source-seeding workflow; it does not imply that no later curation occurred. PROPOSED marks an addition awaiting review; REVIEWED records curator sign-off on identity, structure, filing class and producer claims; DEPRECATED retains a superseded or out-of-scope record. These are workflow decisions, not independently verified human review. Agent-assisted contributions may appear in the curation history below and the record history.

Grounding describes the identifier mapping: EXACT uses an ontology identity asserted by the source; MINTED keeps a local identifier when no defensible ontology identity is available; REVIEW_NEEDED flags an exact structure whose ontology identity needs adjudication. The grounding note retains any unresolved alternatives. These tags do not establish the strength of producer or activity evidence.

A cyclic carotene obtained by dimerisation of all-trans-retinol. A strongly-coloured red-orange pigment abundant in plants and fruit and the most active and important provitamin A carotenoid.

Structure

Standard InChIKeyOENHQHLEOONYIE-JLTXGRSLSA-N

Stereochemistryfully defined
Cross-referencespubchem:5280489

Filing pathway

Terpenoids — computed by NPClassifier npclassifier.gnps2.org@2026-09-07, which is why it is pinned rather than recomputed. The gene cluster's asserted class is terpene, from MIBiG.

Producer organisms 0

None. The compound has a characterized gene cluster whose host NCBI does not name, which for an uncultured symbiont is the honest state — see the discussion below.

Occurrences 25

Somebody found the compound in this organism and cited it. That is not a claim that the organism makes it, and nothing here promotes one to the other.

A locus claim and a taxon claim are different questions. Heterologous expression settles the first and leaves the second where the isolation report left it.

Open questions 6

name-disagreement OPEN controversy — ChEBI calls this structure 'β-carotene' and MIBiG calls it 'β-carotein'. They share a Standard InChIKey, so if the names denote different compounds then one upstream record has the wrong structure. Check which.

unnamed-producer OPEN curation todo — No producer claim is written from BGC0000646, which gives NCBITaxon:77133 (uncultured bacterium — shared by every unplaced bacterium), because the taxon names no single organism. The cluster is kept: a characterized locus is an origin assertion whether or not its host has a name, and for a compound from an uncultured symbiont that is the honest state. Name the producer if the primary literature does.

curator-q7z859-assay-reference OPEN open question — Which CrtYB sequence and isoform were assayed for terminal cyclization, and can that assay be mapped to canonical Q7Z859 E307 rather than CBS6938 K307?

curator-p37295-assay-reference OPEN open question — Which al-2 coding model was used in terminal-cyclization assays, and does it match canonical P37295 602aa rather than the historical partial601aa model?

curator-q43415-assay-reference OPEN open question — Which LCY1 sequence and construct were assayed, and does it match canonical Q43415 498aa rather than Lamuyo T146A/N152K or a processed chain?

curator-dclcyb1-construct-reference OPEN open question — What is the complete Nantaise assay sequence, and how does it map to the B493 reference?
